CORPORATE PROFILE
公司概況
China Foods Limited (“China Foods” or the “Company” and together with its
subsidiaries, the “Group”), a subsidiary of COFCO Corporation (“COFCO”) and the
only focused beverage platform of COFCO, is listed on the main board of The Stock
Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (Stock Code: 506).


The Company mainly manufactures, distributes, markets and sells the Coca-Cola
series products at nineteen provincial-level administrative regions in the PRC through
COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited (“COFCO Coca-Cola”), a 65%-owned joint-venture
with The Coca-Cola Company. COFCO Coca-Cola offers twenty-four brands of products
in ten major types of beverages which include sparkling drink, juice, water, milk
drink, energy drink, tea, coffee, functional nutrition drink, sports drink and plant-
based protein drink.


中國食品有限公司（簡稱「中國食品」或「本公司」
                      ，連同其附屬公司統稱「本集團」）為中
糧集團有限公司（「中糧」）的附屬公司及中糧唯一的專業化飲料業務平台，並於香港聯
合交易所有限公司主板上市（股份代號：506）
                     。


本公司目前主要通過與可口可樂公司合作成立並持有65%權益的中糧可口可樂飲料有
限公司（簡稱「中糧可口可樂」）在國內19個省級行政區域從事生產、配送、推廣和銷售
可口可樂系列產品，向消費者提供包括汽水、果汁、水、乳飲料、能量飲料、茶、咖啡、
功能型營養素飲料、運動飲料及植物蛋白飲料等10大品類、24個品牌的產品。

China Foods and Coca-Cola jointly launched the Dingsheng Programme 「鼎勝計劃」 in the PRC
中國食品與可口可樂共同啟動中國區鼎勝計劃




                                                            In 2023, Coca-Cola China, China Foods
                                                            and Swire Coca-Cola developed a
                                                            “Dingsheng Programme” 「鼎勝計劃」 in
                                                            terms of future development goals and
                                                            strategies. It has coincident goals and
                                                            unified actions, with every effort made to
                                                            enhance market competitiveness and
                                                            status, and achieve rapid business growth
                                                            and profitability improvement.




2023年，可口可樂中國與中國食品、太古可口
可樂就未來發展目標和策略形成「鼎勝計劃」 。
系統統一目標，統一行動，全力提升市場競爭
力和地位，實現業務快速增長和盈利能力提升。

    MAJOR EVENTS IN 2023 2 0 2 3 年 大 事 記




     Smooth integration of the non-sparkling supply platform and enhancing the comprehensive strength of the
     overall supply chain
     順利整合非汽水供應平臺，提升整體供應鏈綜合實力




    As at 1 January 2023, China Foods officially launched its self-production of non-sparkling products, achieving a
    comprehensive upgrade of the supply chain platform, thus providing guarantee for the balanced development of
    COFCO Coca-Cola’s multiple categories.
    2023年1月1日，中國食品正式啟動非汽水產品的自產，實現了供應鏈平臺的全面升級，為中糧可口可樂的多品類
    均衡發展提供了保障。




4   CHINA FOODS LIMITED ANNUAL REPORT 2023
                                                                             MAJOR EVENTS IN 2023 2 0 2 3 年 大 事 記




 Seven plants were awarded National Green Factory Certification
 7家工廠通過國家級綠色工廠認證




19 plants of China Foods were awarded as the Excellent Enterprise in Energy and Water Saving by China Beverage
Industry Association for four consecutive years.

In addition to the original four national green plants (Heilongjiang Plant, Beijing Plant, Inner Mongolia Plant and
Sichuan Plant), three plants, being Qingdao Plant, Jinan Plant, and Gansu Plant, were awarded the title of National
Green Plant in 2023.
中國食品19家工廠連續4年獲得中國飲料協會的節能節水優秀企業稱號。

在原有4家國家級綠色工廠（黑龍江廠、北京廠、內蒙廠、四川廠）的基礎上，2023年，青島、濟南、甘肅三家工廠獲
得國家級綠色工廠稱號。

                                                                     Enterprise and Baosteel Packaging, to improve
                                                                     the operational efficiency and effectively reduce
                                                                     the costs of both parties through sharing

                                                                     用。




6   CHINA FOODS LIMITED ANNUAL REPORT 2023
                                                                           MAJOR EVENTS IN 2023 2 0 2 3 年 大 事 記




 COFCO’s Lohas Changbai Mountain Natural Mineral Water has been launched
 中糧悅活長白山天然礦泉水上市




On 8 April 2023, a release conference was held for the launch of a new product, COFCO “Lohas” Changbai
Mountain natural mineral water, which sources from the original ecological circle of Changbai Mountain, featured
by a natural spring with pure water quality and no pollution. Upon the launch, sales were quickly initiated in
more than 10 markets, with annual sales reaching RMB35.8 million in 2023.
2023年4月8日，中糧「悅活」長白山天然礦泉水新品正式舉行上市發佈會。本次發佈的「悅活」長白山天然礦泉水
取自長白山原生態圈，天然湧泉，水質純淨，無污染。上市後，迅速在10餘個市場啟動銷售，2023年全年銷售額達
到人民幣3,580萬元。

                                                      2022 Industry Most Potential Award jointly issued by the

                                                      Industry Development and Management Center (智能售貨 業
                                                      發展管理中心), the Asia-Pacific Vending Industry Association,
                                                      and the Asia Vending & Smart Retail Expo for its rapid growth

                                                      雙高速增長，榮獲中國商業聯合會、智能售貨產業發展管理

                                                      博覽交易會等聯合頒發的2022年度行業最具潛力獎。

                                                                             管理層論析



CURRENT STATUS                                                                     業務現狀

distribute products under the Coca-Cola series encompasses 19                      圍共計19個省級行政區域，覆蓋中國內地

     MR. QING LIJUN
     Mr. Qing, 54, was appointed the chairman of the board of the Company in September 2022 and has been as
     an executive director and the managing director of the Company since January 2020. Mr. Qing is currently a
     director and the chief executive officer of COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited, a 65%-owned subsidiary of the
     Company, and a director of certain subsidiaries of the Company. Mr. Qing joined COFCO Corporation and/or its
     subsidiaries in July 1993 and has held various positions including the director of strategy department of COFCO
     Corporation during the period from July 2016 to March 2019. Mr. Qing was the deputy general manager of COFCO
     Coca-Cola Beverages Limited during the period from December 2007 to June 2016. Mr. Qing has concurrently
     served as the strategy planning director of COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited during the period April 2004 to
     March 2014. During the period from April 2004 to June 2016, Mr. Qing has also concurrently served various
     positions including the director of the public relations department and the director of the finance department of
     COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited, the general manager of COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages (Shandong) Limited
     and the general manager of COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages (Beijing) Limited. Mr. Qing has served as the deputy
     general manager of Jilin COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited during the period from June 2002 to March 2004
     and a sales operation manager of COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited during the period from April 2000 to May
     2002.

     Mr. Qing graduated from Renmin University of China and Guanghua School of Business Management of Peking
     University with a Bachelor’s degree in Economics and a Master’s degree in Business Administration, respectively.
     Mr. Qing has extensive experience in beverage production, marketing, strategy planning, finance, public relations
     and general management.

MR. CHEN GANG
Mr. Chen, 52, was appointed as a non-executive director of the Company in August 2021. Mr. Chen is currently

Limited, both of which are 65%-owned subsidiaries of the Company. Mr. Chen joined COFCO Corporation in 1994

Limited and COFCO Wine & Spirit (Investment) Limited (both subsidiaries of COFCO Corporation). Mr. Chen has

Industrial Park.

industrial development, international trade, production and processing, brand marketing and technological innovation

     MS. LEUNG KA LAI, ADA, SBS
     Ms. Leung, age 63, was appointed as an independent non-executive director of the Company in June 2022. Ms.
     Leung is currently a member of Standing Commission on Civil Service Salaries and Conditions of Service of the
     HKSAR Government. She joined the HKSAR Government in September 1983 and has served in the civil service
     for 35 years until her retirement in 2019. Ms. Leung joined the Intellectual Property Department (IPD) of the
     HKSAR Government as a senior solicitor in 1995 and was the director of Intellectual Property and Registrar of
     Trade Marks, Patents and Designs since May 2014 until her retirement in March 2019. Prior to joining IPD, Ms.
     Leung was a senior crown counsel with the then Attorney General’s Chambers (now Department of Justice). She
     had worked with both the Litigation Unit and the Advisory Unit in the Civil Division. Ms. Leung was also a part-
     time Lecturer, Faculty of Law, of University of Hong Kong during the period from October 2020 to August 2021.
     Ms. Leung was appointed as an Official Justice of Peace by the HKSAR Government since 2012 and until February
     2020. She was awarded the Silver Bauhinia Star (SBS) by the HKSAR Government in 2018.
     Ms. Leung has over 20 years professional experience in IP law practice as well as financial and staff management,
     management of outsourcing contracts as well as planning and implementing promotion and public education
     programmes in the public sector. She also has extensive experience in formulation of policy, law and practice in
     different areas of intellectual property as well as building and maintaining network with local and international
     stakeholders, including the legal profession, intellectual property practitioners, trade and business organisations,
     overseas and Mainland counterparts and inter-governmental organisations. She has also represented Hong Kong,
     China in different international conferences and meetings in formulating IP policies at the international level. She
     also has experience in civil advisory, civil litigation and criminal prosecution with the then Attorney General’s
     Chambers (now Department of Justice).
     Ms. Leung holds a Bachelor Degree in Science from the University of Hong Kong. She then obtained a Government
     Legal Training Scholarship and studied law in the University of Buckingham in England. She was called to the
     Bar in England and in Hong Kong.

     Risk Management and Internal Control                                    風險管理及內部監控
     The Board acknowledges its responsibility for maintaining               董事會確認其對維持適合及有效的風險管
     appropriate and effective risk management and internal control          理和內部監控制度承擔責任，並通過董事會
     systems, and for reviewing the effectiveness of these systems           審核委員會檢討這些系統的有效性。
     through the audit committee of the Board.

     Sound risk management and internal control systems are based            健全的風險管理和內部監控體系是基於本
     on the Company’s good business ethics and culture, the quality          公司良好的商業道德與文化、員工的素質與
     and capabilities of its employees, the direction of the Board, and      能力、董事會的方向以及管理層的效率與能
     also the efficiency and capabilities of management.                     力。

     Management of the Company, under the Board supervision, is              本公司的管理層在董事會的監督下，負責風
     responsible for the design, implementation and monitoring of the        險管理和內部監控系統的設計，實施和監
     risk management and internal control systems.                           督。

     The Group has established a sound and comprehensive risk                本集團已經建立起完善的全面風險管理和
     management and internal control system, formed a systematic             內部監控體系，形成了科學有效的職責分工
     and effective system on segregation of duty and three lines of          體系，建立了風險管理三道防線，即業務部
     defense in risk management, with each of the business units as          門為第一道防線；各級業務監督檢查部門
     the first line of defense; the business supervision and inspection      為第二道防線；內審部和監察部為第三道
     departments at all levels as the second line of defense; and internal   防線。
     audit department and supervision department as the third line of
     defense.

     The Group has established its risk management and internal control      本集團依照COSO (Committee of Sponsoring
     systems based on the COSO (Committee of Sponsoring Organisation         Organisations of the Treadway Commission)
     of the Treadway Commission) risk management framework.                  的風險管理框架搭建風險管理及內部監控系
                                                                             統。

     The Group’s risk management covers the Company’s headquarters           本集團的風險管理工作覆蓋本公司總部及
     and its subsidiaries, covering various risks faced in operation and     各下屬公司，內容涵蓋經營管理中面臨的各
     management, and focusing on and continuously monitor the key            種風險，並對其中的關鍵風險進行重點關注
     risks.                                                                  和持續監督。

     According to the risk management strategy, the Group conducts           根據風險管理策略，結合本集團的戰略和經
     a comprehensive risk assessment every two years in combination          營目標，以及市場環境的變化，本集團每兩
     with the Group’s strategic and business objectives and changes          年實施一次全面風險評估。識別對實現戰略
     in the market environment. Identify major risks that adversely          目標造成不利影響的主要風險，並根據一套
     affect the achievement of strategic objectives and prioritise the       標準準則評估及排列所識別風險的優先次
     identified risks based on a set of standard criteria and formulate      序，制定風險緩解計劃以便將風險監控至可
     a risk mitigation plan to monitor the risks to an acceptable level.     接受水準。

     The Group conducted a comprehensive risk assessment in 2023.            本集團於2023年進行了一次全面風險評估
     The internal audit department summarised and sorted the                 工作，內審部根據評估結果進行了匯總和
     assessment results, and selected the top five risks with the highest    排序，取評分最高的前五大風險為本集團
     scores as the main risks faced by the Group in 2024, and                2024年面對的主要風險並制定了相應的監
     formulated corresponding monitoring measures. These risks mainly        控措施。該等風險主要涵蓋市場競爭、安全
     cover market competition, safe production, cost pressure and            生產、成本壓力和中美關係等方面。
     China-United States relations.

                                                             intangible assets in the context of the applicable
                                                             financial reporting framework under HKFRSs.
                                                             我們根據香港財務報告準則在適用財務報告框架
                                                             內評估有關商譽及使用壽命不確定的無形資產減
                                                             值評估的綜合財務報表披露。

                                                       Based on the above, we considered that the
                                                       management’s impairment assessment of goodwill and
                                                       the indefinite lives intangible assets were supportable by
                                                       the evidence obtained and procedures performed.
                                                       基於上文所述，我們認為，管理層對商譽及使用壽命不
                                                       確定的無形資產的減值評估可以得到收集的證據及執
                                                       行的程序的支持。

    3.2 Capital management                                                 3.2    資本管理
         The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to                      本集團管理資本之目標為保障
         safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going                     本 集 團 持 續 經 營 之 能 力，以 為
         concern in order to provide returns for shareholders                     股東帶來回報及為其他權益持
         and benefits for other stakeholders and to maintain                      有 人 帶 來 利 益，以 及 維 持 最 佳
         an optimal capital structure to reduce the cost of                       資本結構以減低資本成本。
         capital.

         In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure,                    為 維 持 或 調 整 資 本 結 構，本 集
         the Group may adjust the amount of dividends paid                        團可能會調整支付予股東的股
         to shareholders, provide returns for shareholders, issue                 息金額、為股東提供回報、發行
         new shares or sell assets.                                               新股或出售資產。

         The Group monitors capital on the basis of the gearing                   本集團以資本負債比率監察資
         ratio. This ratio is calculated as total borrowings divided              本。資 本 負 債 比 率 乃 按 借 貸 總
         by total equity. Total equity was shown in the                           額 除 以 權 益 總 額 計 算。權 益 總
         consolidated balance sheet.                                              額於綜合資產負債表內呈列。

           an on-going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are               設進行覆核。如果會計估計的變更僅

           the revision affects only that period, or in the period of the         當期予以確認，或者如果變更對當期

6   SEGMENT REPORTING                                                 6   分部呈報
    The Group’s revenue and consolidated results are mainly               本集團的收入及綜合業績主要來自加
    derived from processing, bottling and distribution of sparkling       工、裝瓶及分銷汽水及不含氣飲料，
    and still beverages, which is regarded as a single operating          該分部被視為單一經營分部，呈報方
    segment in a manner consistent with the nature of the                 式與產品性質及產品加工、根據產品
    products and production process, the types of customers               劃分的客戶類型、用於分銷產品的方
    for their products, the methods used to distribute their              式及監管環境性質一致。因此，並無
    products, and the nature of the regulatory environment.               呈列分部資料，且本集團被視作單一
    Accordingly, no segment information is presented, and the             呈報分部。
    Group is regarded as a single reporting segment.

    Geographical information                                              地域資料
    All revenue of the continuing operation of the Group is               本集團持續經營業務之全部收入源於
    derived from customers operating in Mainland China and                中國內地經營的客戶，以及本集團除
    the Group’s non-current assets, other than deferred income            遞延所得稅項資產外的非流動資產主
    tax assets, are primarily situated in Mainland China, hence           要位於中國內地，故並無根據香港財
    no geographical information is presented in accordance with           務報告準則第8號「經營分部」呈列地
    HKFRS 8 “Operating Segments”.                                         域資料。

    Information about major customers                                     主要客戶資料

      13 INCOME TAX EXPENSE                                                13 所得稅支出
           13.1 Accounting policies of income tax expense                    13.1 所得稅支出的會計政策
                The income tax expense or credit for the period is                期內所得稅支出或抵免為本期
                the tax payable on the current period’s taxable income            應課稅收入項根據各司法轄區
                based on the applicable income tax rate for each                  適用之所得稅率計算之應繳稅
                jurisdiction, adjusted by changes in deferred income              項，並 經 暫 時 差 額 應 佔 遞 延 所
                tax assets and liabilities attributable to temporary              得稅資產及負債變動及未動用
                differences and to unused tax losses.                             稅項虧損調整。

                Current income tax                                               即期所得稅
                The current income tax charge is calculated on the               即期所得稅支出乃根據本公司
                basis of the tax laws enacted or substantively enacted           及其附屬公司及聯營公司經營
                at the end of the reporting period in the countries              及產生應課稅收入之國家於報
                where the company and its subsidiaries and associates            告期末已頒布或實質頒布之稅
                operate and generate taxable income. Management                  法 計 算。管 理 層 就 適 用 稅 務 法
                periodically evaluates positions taken in tax returns            例詮釋所規限之情況定期評
                with respect to situations in which applicable tax               估 報 稅 表 之 狀 況，並 考 慮 稅 務
                regulation is subject to interpretation and considers            機關是否可能接受不確定之稅
                whether it is probable that a taxation authority will            務 處 理。本 集 團 根 據 最 可 能 之
                accept an uncertain tax treatment. The Group                     金額或預期價值計量其稅項結
                measures its tax balances either based on the most               餘，視 乎 哪 種 方 法 能 為 解 決 不
                likely amount or the expected value, depending on                確定性提供更好的預測。
                which method provides a better prediction of the
                resolution of the uncertainty.

                Deferred income tax                                              遞延所得稅
                Deferred income tax is provided in full, using the               遞延所得稅採用負債法就資產
                liability method, on temporary differences arising               及負債之稅基與其於綜合財務
                between the tax bases of assets and liabilities and              報表之賬面值間之暫時差額作
                their carrying amounts in the consolidated financial             出全數撥備。然而，倘遞延所得
                statements. However, deferred income tax liabilities             稅負債來自商譽之初步確認，
                are not recognised if they arise from the initial                則 不 予 確 認。倘 遞 延 所 得 稅 來
                recognition of goodwill. Deferred income tax is also             自在交易（業務合併除外）中對
                not accounted for if it arises from initial recognition          資 產 或 負 債 的 初 步 確 認，而 在
                of an asset or liability in a transaction other than a           交易時不影響會計損益或應課
                business combination that at the time of the transaction         稅 損 益，且 並 無 產 生 等 額 應 課
                affects neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss            稅 及 可 扣 稅 暫 時 差 額，則 不 會
                and does not give rise to equal taxable and deductible           入 賬。遞 延 所 得 稅 採 用 於 報 告
                temporary differences. Deferred income tax is                    期 末 前 已 頒 佈 或 實 質 頒 佈，並
                determined using tax rates (and laws) that have been             預期於變現相關遞延所得稅資
                enacted or substantively enacted by the end of the               產或償還遞延所得稅負債時適
                reporting period and are expected to apply when the              用之稅率（及法例）釐定。
                related deferred income tax asset is realised or the
                deferred income tax liability is settled.

        (continued)
       Deferred income tax (continued)                                      遞延所得稅（續）
       Deferred income tax assets are recognised only if it                 遞延所得稅資產僅會於未來有
       is probable that future taxable amounts will be available            可能出現應課稅溢利以動用該
       to utilise those temporary differences and losses.                   等暫時差額及虧損時確認。

       Deferred income tax liabilities and assets are not                   倘本公司能夠控制撥回暫時差
       recognised for temporary differences between the                     額 之 時 間，且 有 關 差 額 可 能 不
       carrying amount and tax bases of investments in                      會 於 可 見 將 來 撥 回，則 不 會 就
       foreign operations where the company is able to control              海外業務投資之賬面值與稅基
       the timing of the reversal of the temporary differences              間之暫時差額確認遞延所得稅
       and it is probable that the differences will not reverse             負債及資產。
       in the foreseeable future.

       Deferred income tax assets and liabilities are offset                倘有法定可執行權利抵銷即期
       where there is a legally enforceable right to offset                 所 得 稅 資 產 及 負 債，而 遞 延 所
       current income tax assets and liabilities and where                  得稅結餘與同一稅務機關有
       the deferred income tax balances relate to the same                  關，則 可 抵 銷 遞 延 所 得 稅 資 產
       taxation authority. Current income tax assets and tax                及 負 債。當 實 體 有 合 法 可 強 制
       liabilities are offset where the entity has a legally                執行權利抵銷及擬按淨額基準
       enforceable right to offset and intends either to settle             結 算，或 同 時 變 現 資 產 及 結 算
       on a net basis, or to realise the asset and settle the               負 債 時，即 期 所 得 稅 資 產 及 稅
       liability simultaneously.                                            項負債可互相抵銷。

       Current and deferred income tax is recognised in profit              即期及遞延所得稅於損益確
       or loss, except to the extent that it relates to items               認，惟 與 於 其 他 全 面 收 益 或 直
       recognised in other comprehensive income or directly                 接於權益確認之項目有關者則
       in equity. In this case, the tax is also recognised in               除外。在此情況下，稅項亦分別
       other comprehensive income or directly in equity,                    於其他全面收益或直接於權益
       respectively.                                                        中確認。

17 INTANGIBLE ASSETS                                                  17 無形資產
    17.1 Accounting policies of intangible assets                        17.1 無形資產的會計政策
          Goodwill is measured as described in note 18. Goodwill              商 譽 按 附 註18所 述 計 量。收 購
          on acquisitions of subsidiaries is included in intangible           附屬公司之商譽計入無形資
          assets. Goodwill is not amortised but it is tested for              產。商譽不予攤銷，但須每年進
          impairment annually, or more frequently if events or                行 減 值 測 試，或 倘 有 事 件 或 情
          changes in circumstances indicate that it might be                  況 變 動 顯 示 商 譽 可 能 減 值，則
          impaired, and is carried at cost less accumulated                   更 頻 密 地 進 行 減 值 測 試，並 按
          impairment losses. Gains and losses on the disposal                 成 本 減 累 計 減 值 虧 損 列 賬。出
          of an entity include the carrying amount of goodwill                售實體之盈虧包括與出售實體
          relating to the entity sold.                                        有關之商譽賬面值。

          Goodwill is allocated to cash-generating units for the               就 減 值 測 試 而 言，商 譽 會 分 配
          purpose of impairment testing. The allocation is made                至 現 金 產 生 單 位。商 譽 分 配 至
          to those cash-generating units or groups of cash-                    預期可自產生商譽之業務合併
          generating units that are expected to benefit from the               中受惠之現金產生單位或現金
          business combination in which the goodwill arose. The                產 生 單 位 組 別。單 位 或 單 位 組
          units or groups of units are identified at the lowest                別 於 就 內 部 管 理 目 的 而 言，即
          level at which goodwill is monitored for internal                    經 營 分 部，在 商 譽 被 監 察 之 最
          management purposes, being the operating segments                    低層面識別（附註6）     。
          (note 6).

          Intangible assets with finite useful lives that are                  單獨取得的使用年期確定的無
          acquired separately are carried at costs less                        形 資 產，應 按 成 本 減 去 累 計 攤
          accumulated amortisation and any accumulated                         銷額和累計減值損失後的餘額
          impairment losses.                                                   列示。

          Intangible assets with indefinite useful lives acquired             業務合併中取得的並且與商譽
          in a business combination are recognised separately                 分開確認使用壽命不確定之無
          from goodwill and are initially recognised at their fair            形資產按其在購買日的公平值
          value at the acquisition date (which is regarded as                 （即被視為該等無形資產的成
          their cost).                                                        本）進行初始確認。

           17.1 Accounting policies of intangible assets                        17.1 無形資產的會計政策（續）
                (continued)
                The accounting for an intangible asset is based on its             無形資產之會計處理以其使用
                useful life. An intangible asset with a finite useful life         壽 命 為 基 準。具 有 有 限 使 用 壽
                is amortised and an intangible asset with an indefinite            命 的 無 形 資 產 予 以 攤 銷，而 使
                useful life is not.                                                用壽命不確定的無形資產則不
                                                                                   予攤銷。

                Amortisation for intangible assets with finite useful lives        攤銷額在無形資產的固定使用
                is recognised on a straight-line basis over their                  年 期 內 按 直 線 法 確 認。預 計 使
                estimated useful lives. The estimated useful life and              用壽命及攤銷方法會在每一報
                amortisation method are reviewed at the end of each                告 期 末 進 行 覆 核，並 採 用 未 來
                reporting period, with the effect of any changes in                適用法對估計變更的影響進行
                estimate being accounted for on a prospective basis.               核算。

                The Group is required to test an intangible asset with             本集團須透過比較其可收回金
                an indefinite useful life for impairment by comparing              額 與 賬 面 值，對 使 用 壽 命 不 確
                its recoverable amount with its carrying amount:                   定的無形資產進行減值測試：

                (a)   annually, and                                                (a)   每年，及

                (b)   whenever there is an indication that the                     (b)   當有跡象顯示無形資產
                      intangible asset may be impaired.                                  可能出現減值時。

                The useful life of an intangible asset that is not being           未攤銷的無形資產的使用壽命
                amortised shall be reviewed each period to determine               須 於 每 個 期 間 審 查，以 釐 定 事
                whether events and circumstances continue to support               件及情況是否繼續支持對該資
                an indefinite useful life assessment for that asset.               產使用壽命不確定的評估。

                An intangible asset is derecognised on disposal, or                無 形 資 產 在 處 置 時，或 在 使 用
                when no future economic benefits are expected from                 或處置時預計不會產生經濟利
                use or disposal. Gains and losses arising from                     益時，會進行核銷。無形資產在
                derecognition of an intangible asset, measured as the              核銷時產生的利得或損失由計
                difference between the net disposal proceeds and the               量處置所得款項的淨額和資產
                carrying amount of the asset, are recognised in profit             賬 面 價 值 的 差 額 所 得，該 利 得
                or loss when the asset is derecognised.                            或損失計入資產核銷的當期損
                                                                                   益。

             The following table sets out the key assumptions                        下表載列管理層於釐定

           18.1 Accounting policies of business combination                             18.1 業務合併的會計政策
                The acquisition method of accounting is used to                              不論是否收購股本工具或其他
                account for all business combinations, regardless of                         資 產，所 有 業 務 合 併 均 採 用 收
                whether equity instruments or other assets are                               購 會 計 法 入 賬。就 收 購 附 屬 公
                acquired. The consideration transferred for the                              司事項而轉讓之代價包括：
                acquisition of a subsidiary comprises the:

                •     fair values of the assets transferred                                     •      所轉讓資產之公平值

                •     liabilities incurred to the former owners of the                          •      向收購業務前擁有人產
                      acquired business                                                                生之負債

                •     equity interests issued by the group                                      •      本集團發行之股權

                •     fair value of any asset or liability resulting from                       •      或然代價安排產生之任
                      a contingent consideration arrangement, and                                      何資產或負債之公平值，
                                                                                                       以及

                •     fair value of any pre-existing equity interest in                         •      該附屬公司中任何原有
                      the subsidiary.                                                                  的股權之公平值。

       exchange. The discount rate used is the entity’s                     所採用之貼現率為實體之遞增

           21.1 Accounting policies of trade receivables                      21.1 應收貿易款項的會計政策
                 Trade receivables are amounts due from customers                  應收貿易款項為於日常業務過
                 for goods sold in the ordinary course of business.                程中就已售貨品應收客戶之款
                 Trade receivables are recognised initially at the amount          項。應 收 貿 易 款 項 初 步 按 無 條
                 of consideration that is unconditional, unless they               件 代 價 金 額 確 認，除 非 其 包 含
                 contain significant financing components, when they               重 大 融 資 部 分，則 按 公 平 值 確
                 are recognised at fair value. The Group holds the trade           認。本 集 團 持 有 以 收 取 合 約 現
                 receivables with the objective of collecting the                  金流量為目的之應收貿易款
                 contractual cash flows and therefore measures them                項，因此，其後以實際利率法按
                 subsequently at amortised cost using the effective                攤銷成本計量。
                 interest method.

      22 INVENTORIES                                                       22 存貨
           22.1 Accounting policies of inventories                           22.1 存貨的會計政策
                Raw materials and finished goods are stated at the                原材料及製成品按成本與可變
                lower of cost and net realisable value. Cost comprises            現淨值兩者中之較低者列賬。
                direct materials, direct labour and an appropriate                成 本 包 括 直 接 材 料、直 接 勞 工
                proportion of variable and fixed overhead expenditure,            及適當比例的可變及固定間接
                the latter being allocated on the basis of normal                 開 支，後 者 按 正 常 營 運 能 力 分
                operating capacity. Cost excludes borrowing costs.                配。成本不包括借貸成本。成本
                Costs are assigned to individual items of inventory on            按加權平均成本分配至個別存
                the basis of weighted average costs. Costs of purchased           貨 項 目。購 入 存 貨 之 成 本 於 扣
                inventory are determined after deducting rebates and              除 返 利 及 折 扣 後 釐 定。可 變 現
                discounts. Net realisable value is the estimated selling          淨值為日常業務過程中之估計
                price in the ordinary course of business less the                 售價減估計完成成本及進行銷

    ACCOUNTING POLICIES
   39.1 Principles of consolidation and equity accounting               39.1 合併及權益會計原則
        (i)    Subsidiaries                                                  (i) 附屬公司
               Subsidiaries are all entities (including structured               附屬公司指本集團對其
               entities) over which the Group has control. The                   擁有控制權的所有實體
               Group controls an entity where the Group is                       （包括結構性實體）   。當本
               exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns                    集團因參與該實體而承
               from its involvement with the entity and has the                  擔可變回報之風險或享
               ability to affect those returns through its power                 有可變回報之權利，並有
               to direct the activities of the entity. Subsidiaries              能力透過其指揮該實體
               are fully consolidated from the date on which                     活動之權力影響該等回
               control is transferred to the Group. They are                     報，則 本 集 團 控 制 該 實
               deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.                 體。附屬公司自控制權轉
                                                                                 移至本集團當日起全面
                                                                                 綜合入賬。於控制權終止
                                                                                 當日起停止合併入賬。

               Inter-company transactions, balances and                              集團內公司間之交易、結
               unrealised gains on transactions between group                        餘及未變現收益均予以
               companies are eliminated. Unrealised losses are                       對銷。未變現虧損亦予以
               also eliminated unless the transaction provides                       對銷，除非交易有證據顯
               evidence of an impairment of the transferred                          示所轉讓資產出現減值。
               asset. Accounting policies of subsidiaries have                       附屬公司的會計政策已
               been changed where necessary to ensure                                在有需要時作出改變，以
               consistency with the policies adopted by the                          確保與本集團所採納的
               Group.                                                                政策一致。

               Non-controlling interests in the results and                          非控股權益於附屬公司
               equity of subsidiaries are shown separately in                        業績及權益中分別於綜
               the consolidated statement of profit or loss,                         合損益表、全面收益表、
               statement of comprehensive income, statement                          權益變動表及財務狀況
               of changes in equity and statement of financial                       表內呈列。
               position respectively.

        (ii)   Associates                                                    (ii)    聯營公司
               Associates are all entities over which the Group                      聯營公司指本集團對其
               has significant influence but not control or joint                    有重大影響力，但無控制
               control. This is generally the case where the                         權或共同控制權之所有
               Group holds between 20% and 50% of the                                實 體。本 集 團 一 般 持 有
               voting rights. Investments in associates are                          20%至50%表決權。於聯
               accounted for using the equity method of                              營公司之投資於初步按
               accounting (see (iii) below), after initially being                   成本確認後採用權益會
               recognised at cost.                                                   計法（見下文(iii))入賬。

                        Under the equity method of accounting, the                          根據權益會計法，投資初

                        adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s                        調整，以確認本集團應佔
                        share of the post-acquisition profits or losses of                  被投資方的收購事項後

                        share of movements in other comprehensive                           資方的其他全面收益變
                        income of the investee in other comprehensive                       動。已收或應收聯營公司
                        income. Dividends received or receivable from                       股息乃確認為投資賬面
                        associates are recognised as a reduction in the                     值之減少。
                        carrying amount of the investment.

                        Where the Group’s share of losses in an equity-                     倘本集團應佔以權益入
                        accounted investment equals or exceeds its                          賬的投資虧損等於或超
                        interest in the entity, including any other                         過其於該實體的權益（包
                        unsecured long-term receivables, the Group                          括任何其他無抵押長期
                        does not recognise further losses, unless it has                    應收款項），則 本 集 團 不
                        incurred obligations or made payments on behalf                     會確認進一步虧損，除非
                        of the other entity.                                                本集團已代其他實體承
                                                                                            擔責任或作出付款。

                        Unrealised gains on transactions between the                        本集團與其聯營公司之間
                        Group and its associates are eliminated to the                      交易的未實現收益按本集
                        extent of the Group’s interest in these entities.                   團於該等實體的權益為限
                        Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless the                    對銷。未變現虧損亦予以
                        transaction provides evidence of an impairment                      對銷，除非交易有證據顯
                        of the asset transferred. Accounting policies of                    示所轉讓資產出現減值。
                        equity-accounted investees have been changed                        權益入賬被投資方的會計
                        where necessary to ensure consistency with the                      政策已在有需要時作出改
                        policies adopted by the Group.                                      變，以確保與本集團所採

                        The Group treats transactions with non-                             本集團將不導致失去控

                        of control as transactions with equity owners of                    交易視為與本集團權益
                        the Group. A change in ownership interest                           擁有人之交易。擁有權權
                        results in an adjustment between the carrying                       益之變動導致控股權益
                        amounts of the controlling and non-controlling                      及非控股權益之賬面值
                        interests to reflect their relative interests in the                作出調整，以反映其於附
                        subsidiary. Any difference between the amount                       屬公司之相關權益。非控
                        of the adjustment to non-controlling interests                      股權益之調整金額與已
                        and any consideration paid or received is                           付或已收代價之任何差

        and receivable.

        comprehensive income of the subsidiary in the period                 立財務報表之賬面值超過被投
        the dividend is declared or if the carrying amount of                資方資產淨值（包括商譽）於綜

        exceeds the carrying amount in the consolidated                      收 到 該 等 投 資 之 股 息 後，對 該

        Borrowings are initially recognised at fair value, net               借貸初步按公平值扣除所產生
        of transaction costs incurred. Borrowings are                        交 易 成 本 確 認。借 貸 其 後 按 攤

        costs) and the redemption amount is recognised in                    於借貸期間以實際利息法於損

        the effective interest method. Fees paid on the                      之費用於部分或全部融資將很
        establishment of loan facilities are recognised as                   可能提取時確認為貸款之交易

        down. In this case, the fee is deferred until the draw-              跡象顯示該貸款將很可能部分
        down occurs. To the extent there is no evidence that                 或 全 部 提 取 之 情 況 下，該 費 用
        it is probable that some or all of the facility will be              資本化為流動資金服務之預付

        for liquidity services and amortised over the period of
        the facility to which it relates.

        position when the obligation specified in the contract                銷 或 屆 滿 時，借 貸 自 財 務 狀 況

        between the carrying amount of a financial liability                  一方之金融負債之賬面值與已
        that has been extinguished or transferred to another                  付代價（包括任何已轉讓非現金

   39.9 Leases                                                        39.9 租賃
       Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a                 租賃於租賃資產可供本集團使
       corresponding liability at the date at which the leased             用當日確認為使用權資產及相
       asset is available for use by the Group.                            應負債。

       Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease                       合約可包含租賃及非租賃部
       components. The Group allocates the consideration                    分。本 集 團 將 合 約 中 之 代 價 按
       in the contract to the lease and non-lease components                租賃及非租賃部份之相對獨立
       based on their relative stand-alone prices. However,                 價 格 分 配。然 而，就 本 集 團 為
       for leases of real estate for which the Group is a                   承 租 人 之 房 地 產 租 賃 而 言，其
       lessee, it has elected not to separate lease and non-                選擇不將租賃及非租賃部分分
       lease components and instead accounts for these as                   開，而 將 該 等 部 分 入 賬 為 單 一
       a single lease component.                                            租賃部分。

       Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and                租賃條款乃按個別基準磋商，
       contain a wide range of different terms and conditions.              並包含廣泛的不同條款及條
       The lease agreements do not impose any covenants                     件。除 出 租 人 持 有 之 租 賃 資 產
       other than the security interests in the leased assets               之 擔 保 權 益 外，租 賃 協 定 並 無
       that are held by the lessor. Leased assets may not be                附 加 任 何 契 諾。租 賃 資 產 不 得
       used as security for borrowing purposes.                             用作借款的擔保。

       Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially            來自租賃之資產及負債初步按
       measured on a present value basis. Lease liabilities                 現 值 基 準 計 量。租 賃 負 債 包 括
       include the net present value of the following lease                 下列租賃付款之淨現值：
       payments:

       •     fixed payments (including in-substance fixed                   •    固定付款（包括實質上是
             payments), less any lease incentives receivable;                    固定之付款）減任何應收
                                                                                 租賃優惠；

       •     variable lease payments that are based on an                   •    取決於生效之日初始計
             index or a rate, initially measured using the index                 量所使用的指數或比率
             or rate as at the commencement date.                                之可變租賃付款。

       The lease payments are discounted using the interest                 租賃付款按租賃所隱含之利
       rate implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily           率 折 現。倘 該 利 率 無 法 輕 易 確
       determined, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate                  定，則 使 用 承 租 人 的 增 量 借 貸
       is used, being the rate that the individual lessee would             利率，即在條件、擔保和條件相
       have to pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain                  近 的 相 似 經 濟 環 境 中，個 人 承
       an asset of similar value to the right-of-use asset in               租人必須支付借款以獲得與使
       a similar economic environment with similar terms,                   用權資產具有類似價值的資產
       security and conditions.                                             所需的利率。

           39.9 Leases (continued)                                           39.9 租賃（續）
                To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the                  為 釐 定 借 貸 利 率 之 遞 增，本 集
                Group:                                                            團：

                •     uses recent third-party financing received by the          •    以個人承租人最近獲得的
                      individual lessee as a starting point, adjusted to              第三方融資為起點，調整
                      reflect changes in financing conditions since                   以反映自收到第三方融資
                      third party financing was received.                             以來融資條件的變化。

                Lease payments are allocated between principal and               租賃付款在本金及融資成本之
                finance cost. The finance cost is charged to profit or           間 分 配。融 資 成 本 於 租 賃 期 內
                loss over the lease period so as to produce a constant           自 損 益 扣 除，以 就 各 期 間 之 負
                periodic rate of interest on the remaining balance of            債結餘得出固定定期利率。
                the liability for each period.

                Right-of-use assets are measured at cost comprising              使 用 權 資 產 按 成 本 計 量，成 本
                the following:                                                   包括下列各項：

                •     the amount of the initial measurement of lease             •    租賃負債之初步計量金
                      liability;                                                      額；

                •     any lease payments made at or before the                   •    於開始日期或之前作出
                      commencement date less any lease incentives                     之任何租賃付款，減任何
                      received;                                                       已收租賃優惠；

                •     any initial direct costs and                               •    任何初始直接成本；及

                •     restoration costs.                                         •    修復成本。

                Right-of-use assets are generally depreciated over the           使用權資產一般按資產可用年
                shorter of the asset’s useful life and the lease term            限或租期兩者之較短者以直線
                on a straight-line basis.                                        法折舊。

                Payments associated with short-term leases and all               與短期租賃及所有低價值資產
                leases of low-value assets are recognised on a straight-         租賃有關之付款按直線法於損
                line basis as an expense in profit or loss. Short-term           益 確 認 為 開 支。短 期 租 賃 為 租
                leases are leases with a lease term of 12 months or              期為12個月或以下且無購買選
                less without a purchase option.                                  擇權之租賃。

                Lease income from operating leases where the Group               本集團為出租人之經營租賃之
                is a lessor is recognised in income on a straight-line           租金收入按租賃年期以直線法
